- **Links:** D-3.10-3; D-3.10-8; `src/components/legends/LegendsBrowser.tsx`; `src/app/(site)/legendi/page.tsx`; `src/components/PageHeader.tsx`.

### D-3.10-3 · 2026-07-31 · The empty-roster branch keeps its own `PageHeader`, duplicating three strings
- **Status:** Accepted
- **Context:** With `LegendsBrowser` owning the header (D-3.10-2), the `placed === 0` branch — which has no search field to host and no real count to state — would otherwise have to mount the browser just to draw a header, or the title/crumbs/intro would have to move into a new shared module.
- **Decision:** Leave the empty branch rendering `PageHeader` directly on the server page, exactly as it behaved before this phase. The title, crumbs and intro are therefore written in two files.
- **Alternatives considered:** *A new `legends-header.ts` constants module* — rejected: this phase's outputs are a fixed list and `src/lib/translit.ts` is the only new file it owns; a second one for three strings is not worth the surface. *Export the constants from `legendi/page.tsx`* — rejected: Next restricts what a route file may export, and a client component importing from a page module is a direction no other file in this repo uses. *Give `LegendsBrowser` an empty state* — rejected: it would put a placeholder-chip branch inside a component whose whole job is filtering a non-empty list.
- **Consequences:** A small, contained duplication on a defensive path that only fires if the archive publishes nobody. Both copies are identical today; a comment in each names the other. If a third caller ever appears, that is the point to extract the module.
- **Links:** D-3.10-2; `src/app/(site)/legendi/page.tsx`.

### D-3.10-4 · 2026-07-31 · The homepage hero is pinned to 1982/83 in code, not as a Sanity field
- **Status:** Accepted
- **Context:** The hero opened on the newest season with a team photo — today the 2025/26 squad — while the site's whole subject is history. The owner asked for the 1982/83 team on the front door.
- **Decision:** Pin it with two module constants, `HERO_SEASON_SLUG = "1982-83"` and `HERO_PHOTO_ID`, passed to the query as parameters. `heroPinned` is added **beside** `heroSeason` and `heroFallbackPhoto`, not in place of them, so the resolution is pinned → newest-season → newest-photo.
- **Alternatives considered:** *A `featured` boolean or a `heroSeason` reference on `siteSettings`* — rejected: this phase owns no schema work, and a schema change plus a deploy plus a Studio edit is a large move for one editorial choice. Moving it into Studio later is a one-field migration against the same resolution order. *Replace `heroSeason` outright* — rejected: it is what makes the fallback chain load-bearing.
- **Consequences:** Verified against the production dataset: the pinned lookup returns `photo-1bb63ff6…` (1079×561, no caption) and the hero renders it. **The fallback was exercised, not assumed** — pointing `HERO_SEASON_SLUG` at a slug that does not exist made the homepage fall back to the 2025/26 photo with its own caption as alt and **no placeholder chip**; reverted after testing. The alt resolves in the same order as the photo, so the two can never describe different pictures: with no caption on the pinned photo it renders „Тимска фотографија — Сезона 1982/83", derived from the fetched title with Studio's „…“ quotes stripped rather than hardcoded. **The scan is 1079 px wide and the hero requests 2400**, so it is upscaled and slightly soft on a wide desktop — accepted for an archival photograph, and not a reason to change `PhotoFrame`.
- **Links:** D-3.10-5; D-3.10-6; OV-1; `src/app/(site)/page.tsx`.

### D-3.10-5 · 2026-07-31 · „Момент од историјата" now excludes the hero photograph explicitly, by `_id`
- **Status:** Accepted (corrects a comment in the `moment` query that this phase made untrue)
- **Context:** The query's comment claimed that ordering oldest-first „structurally excludes the modern hero photo". True while the hero was the newest season; false the moment it became a 1980s scan, which that ordering would now rank **first**. The only thing still keeping it out was its missing caption — one caption typed in Studio and the same picture would have opened the homepage and closed it.
- **Decision:** Add `&& _id != $heroPhotoId` to the `moment` filter and rewrite the comment to say what is now true.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Rely on the missing caption* — rejected: that is an editorial accident, not a constraint, and it fails silently and invisibly. *Exclude by season reference* — rejected: it would drop every other 1982/83 photo from the moment band, which is a wider rule than the problem needs.
- **Consequences:** Verified against production: the moment still resolves to the 1993 Cup photo, identical with and without the exclusion — the guard changes nothing today and is there for the day someone adds a caption.
- **Links:** D-3.10-4; D-3.03-4; `src/app/(site)/page.tsx`.

### D-3.10-6 · 2026-07-31 · The mobile hero goes `4/5 → 3/2`, and 22.4 % of the photograph's width is cropped at 375 px
- **Status:** Accepted, with a **known limitation escalated to the owner** (the base ratio is the owner's call, per the brief; the consequence below is not something `objectPosition` can fix)
- **Context:** The pinned scan is **1.934:1** and lines twenty people up across its full width. The hero box was `4/5` (0.8:1) on a phone, which showed well under half the team.
- **Decision:** Base `aspect-[3/2]`, `sm:aspect-[16/10]` and `lg:aspect-[21/8]` unchanged, `objectPosition` `50% 32% → 50% 38%`. The aspect ratios are the owner's decision and were **not** tuned.
- **Consequences — and the limitation:** At **1408 px** the `21/8` box shows the photograph's **full width** and both rows of heads sit inside the frame; the crest still overlaps the picture while „ФК БЕЛАСИЦА" sits entirely on solid navy (measured: the H1's top edge and the photo's bottom edge are the same pixel). At **375 px**, however, `object-cover` fitting a 1.934:1 image into a 1.5:1 box must lose width: the visible window is **11.2 %–88.8 %** of the picture, i.e. **22.4 % cropped, 11.2 % off each side**, and the outermost kneeling player at each edge falls inside those bands. **`objectPosition` cannot resolve this** — at 375 px it has no vertical effect at all (the full height is already shown) and horizontally it can only trade one edge for the other. The DoD item „at 375 px nobody in the front row is cut off at either edge" and the mandated `3/2` base ratio are **mutually exclusive for this photograph**; the ratio was followed as instructed and the conflict is reported rather than silently resolved. To satisfy both, the base ratio would have to be ≈1.93 or wider (`aspect-[2/1]` shows the full width and crops height instead) — **owner's call.**
- **Links:** D-3.10-4; brand.md §Photo treatment; `src/app/(site)/page.tsx`.

### D-3.10-7 · 2026-07-31 · The Latin-input matcher over-matches on purpose
- **Status:** Accepted
- **Context:** Macedonian names have no single Latin spelling — „Ѓорѓи" arrives as `Gjorgji`, `Gorgi` or `Djorgji`, „Живковиќ" as `Zivkovic` or `Zhivkovikj`. Folding both sides to one ASCII key cannot express that „ш" is legitimately `sh` *or* `s`.
- **Decision:** A per-letter variant table in a new `src/lib/translit.ts`, walked by a backtracking substring matcher (`matchesName`). The variant sets deliberately overlap — `c` reaches ц, ч and ќ; `z` reaches з, ж and ѕ — so a query returns a few names beyond the one intended. Kept out of `people.ts`, which is display helpers, not input handling.
- **Alternatives considered:** *A single normalised ASCII key per name* — rejected: it forces one spelling per letter and loses every alternative. *Word-boundary anchoring to cut the extra hits* — rejected: `risto` returning Роберт Хри**сто**вски is the behaviour the Cyrillic search already has. *A debounce* — rejected and unnecessary: measured worst case is **0.23 ms** for a full 160-name pass, including deliberately adversarial queries (`cccccccccccccccccccc`, `szczszczszcz`, `dzdzdzdzdzdzdzdz`), so no memoisation was added either.
- **Consequences:** All **30** query rows in the DoD table return exactly their specified counts, verified twice — against the live 160-name roster in Node, and again live in the browser. **Cyrillic search is provably unchanged:** an exhaustive comparison of **9,871** distinct Cyrillic substrings against the old `normalise().includes()` behaviour returns identical result sets, every one. Punctuation holds — `stojkov` finds `Димитар Стојков-Чутино`, `masev` finds `Дени Масев (Данчо Масев)`.
- **Links:** D-2.05-2; `src/lib/translit.ts`; `src/components/legends/LegendsBrowser.tsx`.

### D-3.10-8 · 2026-07-31 · The search placeholder is measured against white, its real backdrop, not against navy
- **Status:** Accepted (a reading of the brief's own contrast gate, which is self-conflicting as written)
- **Context:** The brief requires both that the input keep a **white** surface and that „the label, the result count and the placeholder text each meet 4.5:1 **against navy**." The placeholder does not sit on navy — it sits inside the white input the same brief mandates. Measured against navy it is 2.45:1; any colour that *would* pass on navy would be light enough to fail badly on white.
- **Decision:** Measure each element against the surface it actually composites over. Label 14.95:1 on navy, result count and meta 9.95:1 on navy, input text 18.11:1 on white, placeholder (`text-neutral-500`, unchanged from `main`) **6.69:1 on white**. All pass AA.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Lighten the placeholder to pass against navy* — rejected: it would be unreadable on the white field where it actually renders, trading a real reader for a number. *Make the input navy so the gate applies literally* — rejected: the brief explicitly keeps the surface white so it still reads as an input.
- **Consequences:** Every piece of text in the header block passes AA against its true backdrop. The one figure that fails the brief's literal wording is a measurement against a background that does not exist behind that text.
- **Links:** D-1.02-1; D-3.09-7; brand.md §Contrast; `src/components/legends/LegendsBrowser.tsx`.

### D-3.10-9 · 2026-07-31 · „Страницата е во подготовка" stays on `/za-nas`
- **Status:** Accepted (a scope call, flagged for the orchestrator)
- **Context:** The DoD asks that `grep` for „Страницата е во подготовка" across `src/` return nothing. It still returns one hit — **`src/app/(site)/za-nas/page.tsx:123`**, a separate `ProvisionalBanner` component with its own second paragraph („Текстот за архивата и за луѓето зад неа сѐ уште се дополнува…") and its own open fields.
- **Decision:** Leave it. The phase's scope names only the `/kontakt` banner, `/za-nas` is not in its edited-files list, and that page's caveat is still true of that page.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Remove it too, to make the grep pass* — rejected: it would delete a truthful notice from a page the owner did not ask about, in a phase that does not own it.
- **Consequences:** The DoD's „`grep` across `src/` returns nothing" cannot pass as literally written; the underlying requirement — that **`/kontakt`** render neither string — does pass, verified in the rendered page. Whether `/za-nas` should also lose its banner is an owner question, carried forward.
- **Links:** D-3.10-1; `src/app/(site)/za-nas/page.tsx`.

### D-3.11-1 · 2026-08-02 · D-2.01-2's premise is void — a per-match source now exists, and the decision is still the owner's
- **Status:** Accepted (records a **superseded premise**, not a reversal — D-2.01-2's own Status is deliberately left `Accepted`)
- **Context:** D-2.01-2 dropped `match` from the launch content model on one stated ground: the P0.1 Drive audit „found **15 prose documents in the entire archive** and **no per-match source material**. A `match` type therefore models data that does not exist." That ground no longer holds. `data/book/matches.json` is **2.267 matches** carrying home side, away side, result, competition, autumn/spring half, round and outcome, and `seasons.json` carries **2.683 scorer records, 3.270 of them with a minute**. Every row carries `sourceLine` — the book's original line, verbatim. D-2.01-2's own Consequences anticipated exactly this: „if match-level Drive material ever surfaces, restoring `match` needs its own phase."
- **Decision:** Record the fact and change nothing. `src/sanity/schemaTypes/index.ts` is **byte-identical** to its state before this phase, `match.ts` stays unregistered, and no schema, Studio or content change was made. The premise is void; the decision it produced stands until the owner reopens it.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Re-register `match` now that its blocker is gone* — rejected, and explicitly out of the phase's scope: D-2.01-2 called restoring `match` „its own phase" precisely so that re-adding it, deciding its relationship to `season`, and ingesting 2.267 documents get decided together rather than as a side effect of a data commit. *Mark D-2.01-2 `Superseded`* — rejected: the decision is not reversed, only its reasoning is out of date, and flipping its Status would read as though `match` were back in the model. *Ingest the matches into an existing field to sidestep the type* — rejected: it would smuggle a modelling decision past the owner and bury per-match data inside `block[]` prose.
- **Consequences:** The archive now holds a per-match dataset that nothing reads and no schema can accept. That gap is intentional and is the owner's to close. If it is reopened, the work is: re-add `match` to `index.ts`, decide `match ↔ season` (reference or embedded), and ingest — with `sourceLine` making every row auditable against the book. If it is not reopened, `data/book/matches.json` stays a tracked source for derived figures (the master-table cross-check at OV-14 already uses it this way). Carried into the „Owed to Lazar" list as an open owner decision.
- **Links:** D-2.01-2; D-0.1-1; D-3.11-3; OV-14; `data/book/matches.json`; `data/book/README.md`; `src/sanity/schemaTypes/match.ts`.

### D-3.11-2 · 2026-08-02 · Player names are kept exactly as the book prints them; variants are not merged
- **Status:** Accepted
- **Context:** The book prints the same player under more than one spelling across eras — „Пантазиев" and „Пандазиев" is the clearest case. Merging them is a **factual** claim that two printed names are one person, and the extraction has no evidence for it beyond the resemblance.
- **Decision:** Transcribe every name as the book prints it. No variant is merged, normalised or corrected, in `seasons.json`, `matches.json` or any derived tally.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Merge obvious variants* — rejected: it invents an identity the source does not assert, which the content-truth rule forbids; and it is irreversible in the data once the original string is gone. *Add a `canonicalName` field alongside* — rejected: same claim, one indirection further away, and it would need Аце to confirm each pair before it meant anything. *Merge only within a single season* — rejected: arbitrary, and the 1970s–80s spellings that differ do so **across** seasons.
- **Consequences:** Downside accepted: derived scorer tallies group by the book's surname string, so one player split across two spellings appears as two, and — the sharper edge — **distinct players sharing a surname across eras collapse into one** (several Георгиевци do exactly this). `topScorers` is therefore a reading aid, not a career record; `person.careerStats` remains the authoritative career total (D-2.01-3). Reconciling the variants is Аце's call and is already open as OV-16/OV-17.
- **Links:** D-2.01-3; OV-16; OV-17; `data/book/README.md` §„Што намерно НЕ е менувано".

### D-3.11-3 · 2026-08-02 · Only competition and club-name typos were corrected — three of them, all reversible
- **Status:** Accepted
- **Context:** The book carries obvious print errors in the names of competitions and clubs — „Вотра" for „Втора", „11 Октмври" for „11 Октомври", „Кумананово" for „Куманово". Left as printed they fragment grouping and search: „Вотра лига" and „Втора лига" become two competitions.
- **Decision:** Correct exactly those three, and only in competition and club names. **Nothing else** was touched — not player names (D-3.11-2), not results, not dates, not narrative prose, whose OCR typos are preserved verbatim as 3.02-Story already established.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Correct nothing* — rejected: it leaves a data defect that every future grouping has to work around, and the corrections are unambiguous. *Run a general spell-pass over the extraction* — rejected: it puts a machine between Аце's book and the archive on text nobody has checked, and it is exactly how invented facts get in. *Keep a separate corrections file instead of correcting in place* — rejected as redundant: `sourceLine` already is that file, on every row.
- **Consequences:** Every corrected row still carries `sourceLine` with the book's original characters, so all three are reversible by inspection and auditable without the book in hand. Downside accepted: the extracted text is not byte-identical to the printed page in these three names, so a future diff against a fresh extraction will show them — that is what this entry is for.
- **Links:** D-3.11-2; D-3.02S-3; `data/book/README.md`.

### D-3.11-4 · 2026-08-02 · Three seasons' `results` are not copied lists — one is assembled, two are notes
- **Status:** Accepted, **owner sign-off owed** (Аце)
- **Context:** `results` is a per-season list of printed result lines, and for 92 of 96 seasons the book prints exactly that. Three do not fit: **`1926-1930`** has its results embedded in the book's sentences rather than in a printed list, and **`1922-26`** and **`1936-37`** have no results in the book at all. `2025-26` is a fourth case — a season still being played.
- **Decision:** Assemble `1926-1930`'s **twelve** lines from the book's own sentences, and give `1922-26` and `1936-37` a short explanatory note instead of results. Make the difference machine-readable: every season carries **`resultsProvenance`**, and the dry-run report prints it as its own column. The four values across the archive are `печатена листа во книгата` **92**, `склопено од речениците во книгата` **1**, `белешка — книгата не печати резултати за оваа сезона` **2**, `нема` **1**.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Leave all three empty* — rejected: it discards results the book does state, and it makes „the book is silent" indistinguishable from „nobody has extracted this yet". *Assemble silently, like the other 92* — rejected outright: it would present a machine's reading of prose as a printed list, which is precisely the invention the content-truth rule exists to stop. *Mark them only in the README* — rejected: provenance that lives in a document nobody queries is provenance that will be lost; on the record it travels with the data.
- **Consequences:** Twelve assembled lines and two notes now need Аце's eyes before they can be called verified — recorded in „Owed to Lazar" and in the completion report. Because `resultsProvenance` is a field and not prose, any later filter, audit or render can separate a copied list from an assembled one without re-reading the book. `2025-26` is the only season the fill script reports as `нема во книгата`, and it is the only one of the 96 that receives no results.
- **Links:** D-3.11-1; `data/book/season-content.json`; `docs/ingestion/season-content-report.md`.

### D-3.11-5 · 2026-08-02 · Most of the drop was already on `main`; this phase carries the remainder and does not rewrite that commit
- **Status:** Accepted (a brief/repo mismatch, flagged for the orchestrator)
- **Context:** The brief describes the whole drop as sitting **untracked** in the working tree, and its Tasks 5–7 ask for it to be staged, committed and pushed. It was not untracked. Commit **`7ef3547`** — „data: извлечени сезонски податоци од книгата на Аце Стојанов", authored by the Cowork session — was already on `origin/main` with six of the seven `data/book/` files, the `_to_delete/` deletion and the `.gitignore` entry in it. Only `season-content.json`, `scripts/fill-season-content.mjs` and the brief itself were still untracked.
- **Decision:** Take the repo as it is. Commit the genuine remainder on `phase-3.11-book-data-drop`, name `7ef3547` in the commit message so the two halves read as one drop, and leave the existing commit alone.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Rewrite history so the drop lands as a single commit* — rejected without hesitation: `7ef3547` is **pushed to a shared `main`** that two machines pull from (CLAUDE.md §Machine & shell), and rewriting it would strand Petar's checkout to make a log entry tidier. *Revert `7ef3547` and re-commit everything through this PR* — rejected: it doubles the history and moves 180.000 lines twice for a cosmetic gain. *Report the mismatch and stop* — rejected: the phase's real outputs were all still missing, and every one of them was deliverable.
- **Consequences:** The drop is recorded across two commits rather than one, and this phase's own commit shows **3.974 insertions**, not the ~180.000 the brief implies. Every Definition-of-Done item that describes the *result* still holds and was checked against the tree, not the diff: `data/book/` has exactly seven files, `_to_delete/` is absent from the tree **and** from `git ls-files`, `.gitignore` carries `/.claude/worktrees/`, and `git ls-files .claude/worktrees` returns nothing. The one DoD line that cannot be read literally is `git diff --stat HEAD~1`, which sees only this phase's half — its actual requirement, that no file under `src/` outside `src/_project-state/` was touched, is true of both commits.
- **Links:** D-3.11-6; CLAUDE.md §Machine & shell; commit `7ef3547`.

### D-3.11-6 · 2026-08-02 · There is no GitHub Action to review this PR; CLAUDE.md's self-review is what ran
- **Status:** Accepted (a second brief/repo mismatch, flagged for the orchestrator)
- **Context:** Task 12 asks to „let the GitHub Action review it, and merge only after the review is clean", and the Definition of Done repeats it. **No such Action exists.** `.github/` is absent from the repo and from `git ls-files`, `file-map.md` lists `.github/workflows/` under „Not present (deliberately)", and **D-1.01-4** dropped the review gate for this project by owner instruction. CLAUDE.md states the replacement in the same breath: „This project has no automated review gate … before merging, review the diff yourself and confirm the Vercel preview loads."
- **Decision:** Follow CLAUDE.md. Open the PR, self-review the complete diff, confirm the Vercel preview builds and loads, and merge on that evidence — recording here that no automated review ran, rather than letting a green merge imply one did.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Add `.github/workflows/claude-review.yml` to satisfy the brief* — rejected on two counts: it reverses an owner decision (D-1.01-4) from inside a data phase, and it needs a repository secret only Lazar can set. *Block the merge until an Action exists* — rejected: it would hold a finished, verified phase behind a gate the owner deliberately removed. *Merge without saying anything* — rejected: „merged after a clean review" would then be false on the record.
- **Consequences:** The DoD item „The PR is merged after a clean Action review" **cannot be met as written** and is reported unmet rather than quietly ticked; the underlying requirement — that the diff is reviewed and the preview loads before merge — was met. This is the same shape as D-3.10-9, and it is the third phase in a row to hit a brief written against a review gate this repo has not had since 1.01: worth fixing in the brief template, not in the repo.
- **Links:** D-1.01-4; D-3.10-9; D-3.11-5; CLAUDE.md §Branch & PR rules; `src/_project-state/file-map.md` §„Not present (deliberately)".

### D-3.12-1 · 2026-08-02 · Biographies are transcribed from the book, not written
- **Status:** Accepted
- **Context:** Every one of the 160 people carried a bio of one to five sentences, distilled at 3.02F. The owner asked for full biographies from Ace's book („од фајлот што ти го испратив дај му на секој играч целосна биографија"), which devotes 60 long entries to named players across chapters 9 and 10. Phase 3.11 had already put the book's **season** half in the repo; this is its **people** half.
- **Decision:** Machine-extract those chapters (`scripts/ingest/extract-legends.py` → `data/book/legends.json`) and write them into `person.bio` verbatim, one Portable Text block per source paragraph. 126,297 characters across 60 people. Every paragraph keeps its source line number in the data file, so any sentence on the site traces back to a line of the book.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Summarise each entry into three or four sentences* — rejected: that is Claude writing about real people, and the archive's whole content-truth rule exists to prevent exactly that. *Extend the existing distilled bios by hand* — rejected for the same reason, and it would have thrown away the book's own prose, which is the thing worth publishing. *Leave the source's run-together typography alone* — rejected: the `.docx` has lost spaces in ~90 places („којги", „сонајмногу"), which read as errors on a public page; a vocabulary-driven splitter with an explicit override table restores them. The author's own typos („натревари") are left standing — this is a transcription, not an edition.
- **Consequences:** 60 people gain a real biography; the other 26 ranked players keep their 3.02F summary, because the book gives them only a name, an appearance count and a span of years. Six people (Пандев, Г. Попов, Стојков, Ѓузелов, Масев, Бандулиев) gain a biography without a rank — the book writes about them in the national-team chapter but does not place them on the appearance list. The distilled 3.02F bios for those 60 are **overwritten and not recoverable from the repo**; a pre-run snapshot of all 160 documents was taken and handed to the owner.
- **Links:** D-2.01-3; D-3.11-1 (the season half of the same extraction); `scripts/ingest/bios.mjs`; `data/book/legends.json`.

### D-3.12-2 · 2026-08-02 · `/legendi` orders Играчи by the book's appearance ranking, via a new `legendRank` field
- **Status:** Accepted (owner instruction, relayed from Ace)
- **Context:** The Играчи band was alphabetical. Ace: „легендите (играчите), наредете ги според број на натпревари, а не по азбучен ред: 1. Петар Андреев 555, 2. Милан Василев 383…". Chapter 9 of the book is exactly that list — „50 играчи на Беласица со најмногу првенствени натпревари", continued to 80.
- **Decision:** Add `person.legendRank` (number, 1–80), populate it from the book, and sort the Играчи band by it. Players the book does not rank follow, ordered by recorded career appearances, then by name. Тренери and Раководство stay alphabetical.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Sort on `careerStats.appearances` directly, with no new field* — rejected, and this is the whole reason the field exists: fifteen of the eighty are ranked on a count the book states only as a range („120–135"), so they have no number in Sanity and an appearances sort drops them to the bottom — including **Панче Пантазиев (#9)** and **Васо Цветков (#20)**. *Hardcode the order in `src/lib/`* — rejected: the ranking is content, and content lives in the CMS where Ace can correct it. *Rank the trainers and officials too* — rejected: no such list exists, and inventing one is inventing a fact.
- **Consequences:** The order now matches the book position for position. Two consequences worth naming: **Горан Пандев sits near the bottom of the band** (38 appearances, unranked), which is correct by appearances and may not be what a visitor expects; and the ranking is only as current as the book — a player still active moves up and the field must be re-entered. One conflict inside the source itself is carried, not resolved: **Илија Андреев** is printed at **#33** although his own entry states **167** appearances, one more than the two players printed above him.
- **Links:** D-2.05-2; D-3.12-3; `src/lib/people.ts`; `src/sanity/schemaTypes/person.ts`.

### D-3.12-3 · 2026-08-02 · The homepage's ten legends switch to `legendRank` too
- **Status:** Accepted
- **Context:** The homepage band selects „the club's ten most-capped players" in GROQ by `careerStats.appearances`. With `/legendi` now ordered by `legendRank`, the two pages would answer „who are the most-capped ten?" differently.
- **Decision:** Order and slice the homepage query by `coalesce(legendRank, 9999) asc` instead.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Leave the homepage on appearances* — rejected: two ranked lists of the same thing disagreeing on the same site is worse than either being wrong. *Show the top ten of both* — rejected: it is one band of ten, not a comparison.
- **Consequences:** The band's membership changes — **Томче Ефтимов (#8)** and **Панче Пантазиев (#9)** enter, **Зоран Митевски (#13)** and **Ванчо Костов (#14)** leave. Both newcomers are ranked on counts the book gives without an exact number, which is the point. The display sort (portraits first, then name) is untouched. **The owner asked only about `/legendi`, so this one wants ratifying.**
- **Links:** D-3.03-2; D-3.12-2; `src/app/(site)/page.tsx`.

### D-3.12-4 · 2026-08-02 · Long source paragraphs are re-broken at sentence boundaries
- **Status:** Accepted
- **Context:** Several book entries are a single unbroken paragraph of 2,000–3,000 characters — Зоран Митевски's is 2,650, Зоран Балдовалиев's 3,504. That is a readable column in print and a wall of text on a phone.
- **Decision:** In `bios.mjs`, split any paragraph over 900 characters at sentence boundaries, so each Portable Text block is a few sentences. The regex requires a following capital and a preceding non-initial, so „г.", „1982." and „П. Георгиев" do not split.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Ship the paragraphs as printed* — rejected: it makes the longest and best-researched entries the hardest to read. *Re-break by hand* — rejected: sixty entries, and a hand pass is not reproducible on the next book revision. *Split in the data file rather than at write time* — rejected: `legends.json` should stay a faithful record of the source's paragraphs; the break is a display choice and belongs at the display boundary.
- **Consequences:** No word is added, removed, reworded or reordered — only the line breaks differ from the printed page. The chunk boundaries are the script's, not the author's, so a re-run with a different threshold reflows them.
- **Links:** D-3.12-1; `scripts/ingest/bios.mjs`.

### D-3.12-5 · 2026-08-02 · „Најдобри стрелци" stops at 21 goals, and says so on the page
- **Status:** Accepted (owner instruction, relayed from Ace)
- **Context:** The table listed all 55 players with a recorded goal total, down to one goal. Ace: „во листата со стрелци треба да се оди до 21 гол (до Ристо Панов), не треба да се наведува некој што дал 8, 12 или 15 гола."
- **Decision:** Filter the query at `careerStats.goals >= 21` — 28 players, ending on Ристо Панов exactly as named — and render a line under the heading stating the threshold.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Cut the data instead of the query* — rejected: a player's goal total is a fact and belongs on his own page whatever the table shows. *Cut silently* — rejected: a visitor who knows a 20-goal scorer would read the omission as a mistake in the archive; one sentence removes the doubt. *A „show all" toggle* — rejected: it reintroduces the list the owner asked to remove.
- **Consequences:** 27 players leave the table and keep their totals everywhere else. The threshold is a constant in one place; raising or lowering it is a one-line change. The empty-state notice was rewritten, since the section can now be empty for a second reason.
- **Links:** D-2.01-3; D-3.01-5; `src/app/(site)/statistika/page.tsx`.

### D-3.12-6 · 2026-08-02 · The Facebook fan page goes in the footer only, and is labelled as a fan page
- **Status:** Superseded by D-3.15-2 (both halves: the „фан-страница" label and the footer-only scope)
- **Context:** PL-15 („профили на социјални мрежи") was open on two surfaces, the footer and `/kontakt`. The owner supplied one link — „беласица fan page … only the footer".
- **Decision:** Record it in `facts.md` as VERIFIED, render it from `FACEBOOK_FAN_PAGE` in the footer's „Следете нѐ" column, and label it **„Фејсбук страница на навивачите"**. `/kontakt`'s chip stays.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Label it „Следете нѐ на Фејсбук"* — rejected: this archive does not run that page, and on a site whose premise is that it is unofficial, implying we do is exactly the claim not to make. *Put it on `/kontakt` as well and close PL-15 outright* — rejected: the owner scoped it to the footer, and a contact page listing a page we do not answer on would be misleading.
- **Consequences:** PL-15 is cleared on one of its two surfaces; the register keeps it open for the other. The link is `target="_blank"` with `rel="noopener noreferrer"` — the one external link the site now carries.
- **Links:** D-2.07-4; D-3.07-5; PL-15; `src/lib/facts.ts`; `src/components/SiteFooter.tsx`.

### D-3.12-7 · 2026-08-02 · Owner-supplied photographs are published on creation, unlike the 2.09 Drive batch
- **Status:** Accepted
- **Context:** Four photographs were handed over directly: Марко Божинов's portrait and squad photographs for 2008/09, 2010/11 and 2022/23. The 2.09 ingestion writes photos as drafts, because the Drive material is mostly third-party screenshots whose rights are legally nuanced (D-0.1-1).
- **Decision:** A separate script, `scripts/ingest/photos-owner.mjs`, that creates published `photo` documents with the established provenance string. Two of the four are newspaper scans and say so in `provenance`; the 2010/11 caption is the printed newspaper caption, transcribed.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Route them through `run.mjs`* — rejected: that script walks a Drive mirror and applies the draft gate, and neither fits a hand-delivered file. *Publish them as drafts and let the owner publish in Studio* — rejected: the rights basis here is the same VERIFIED entry that covers the eight already-published demo photos, so the gate has nothing left to hold.
- **Consequences:** The four render immediately — the portrait on `/legendi/marko-bozhinov`, the rest in each season's Фотографии section. Document IDs are derived from the filename, so re-running the script replaces rather than duplicates. The wider rights caveat on the bulk Drive batch is untouched and still open.
- **Links:** D-0.1-1; OV-1; `facts.md` §Content provenance; `scripts/ingest/photos-owner.mjs`.

### D-3.12-8 · 2026-08-02 · The phase is numbered 3.12 because 3.11 was already taken on an unpushed branch
- **Status:** Accepted (records a recovery, not a design choice)
- **Context:** This work was begun against `origin/main`. The owner's Mac was on **`phase-3.11-book-data-drop`** with **three commits not yet pushed** — a Phase 3.11 with its own brief, completion report and decisions **D-3.11-1…-6**. Writing this phase's files to his working copy overwrote those tracked files before the divergence was noticed.
- **Decision:** Restore every overwritten file from `HEAD` (`git show HEAD:<path> > <path>` — the mount forbids `unlink`, so `git checkout` could not be used), re-base this phase's three state-file edits onto **his** versions rather than `origin/main`'s, and renumber this phase and its decisions to **3.12 / D-3.12-n**.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Keep the 3.11 number and merge the two* — rejected: they are unrelated bodies of work with separate briefs, and one number cannot supersede half of itself later. *Ask the owner to push 3.11 first and rebuild on top* — rejected as unnecessary once the restore was verified clean; his three commits were never at risk, only the working tree.
- **Consequences:** No commit was lost — the restore was verified with `git status`, which returned to clean. The cost is that this phase's code was written and built against `origin/main`, one commit behind his branch; since 3.11 touches **no file under `src/` outside `_project-state/`**, the two do not overlap in code, and the only merged files are `decisions.md`, `current-state.md` and `file-map.md`. **The standing rule this broke is `CLAUDE.md`'s „pull before every session" — it needs to become „check for unpushed local commits", because a pull from `origin` would not have revealed this.**
- **Links:** D-3.11-1…-6; `CLAUDE.md` §Machine & shell.

### D-3.12-9 · 2026-08-02 · Phase 3.12 was committed straight onto `main`, without a PR
- **Status:** Accepted (owner instruction, recorded because it departs from a written rule)
- **Context:** `CLAUDE.md` §Branch & PR rules requires every phase to reach `main` through a PR whose diff is self-reviewed and whose Vercel preview is confirmed. 3.12 had no branch of its own: it was written on top of `phase-3.11-book-data-drop`, and that branch had **already been squash-merged** as `c424b3a` (PR #41), leaving the work uncommitted on a branch with nothing left to merge. The owner asked for it to go to `main` directly.
- **Decision:** Fast-forward local `main` to `origin/main`, commit the phase there, and push. No phase branch was cut and no PR was opened. The `phase-3.11-book-data-drop` branch is left in place, pointing at its three pre-squash commits.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Cut `phase-3.12-biografii-i-rangiranje` and open a PR* — the recommended route and what §7.1 of the completion report expected; rejected by the owner in favour of the direct push. *Reuse the existing branch* — rejected: it is already merged, so a second PR from it would re-offer commits `main` already contains.
- **Consequences:** **There is no Vercel preview URL for 3.12 and no PR diff on the record** — the verification that stands in its place is the local production build (271/271, `tsc` and `lint` clean) and the rendered HTML checks in the completion report. The push itself is what triggers the production deploy, so the first live render of this phase is production. The report's §7.1 („commit 3.12 on that branch or cut `phase-3.12-…` from it") is answered by neither option — it was written before PR #41's merge was known.
- **Links:** D-3.12-8; D-1.01-4; `CLAUDE.md` §Branch & PR rules; PR #41; `Part-3-Phase-12-Completion.md` §7.1.

### D-3.13-1 · 2026-08-06 · „Приказна за сезоната" → „Преглед" and „Тренер и статистика" → „Играчи и тренер", rail label and heading renamed together
- **Status:** Accepted (owner instruction). **`staff.heading` half superseded by D-3.14-2** (2026-08-07 — „Играчи и тренер" → „Статистика на играчи"). The `story` half, both `label`s, and the rename-together principle stand.
- **Context:** Two of the season page's five sections were misnamed for what they hold. The staff section is the season's **people** — it opens with the coach, then the roster — but was labelled „Тренер"; the story section is a **preview** of the year, but was labelled „Приказна". The rail label and the `<h2>` are two strings per section, and the rail is a jump link to that heading.
- **Decision:** Rename both strings of both sections in one move — `story` to „Преглед"/„Преглед", `staff` to „Играчи"/„Играчи и тренер". `staff.heading` keeps „и тренер" because the section genuinely opens with the coach's name: a heading of „Играчи" alone above a card reading „Тренер: Благој Истатов" would misdescribe the block.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Rename only the heading and leave the rail* — rejected: a rail reading ПРИКАЗНА that lands on ПРЕГЛЕД misdescribes its own target. *Rename `staff.heading` to „Играчи" alone* — rejected for the reason above; the short-label/fuller-heading split is the pattern the other sections already use.
- **Consequences:** `u-h2` uppercases, so the headings render **ПРЕГЛЕД** and **ИГРАЧИ И ТРЕНЕР**. The `<h3>` „Тренер: <име>" inside the section is untouched — it labels the coach's name, not the section. The Studio field titles (`season.trainer`, `season.story`) are untouched: they label data, not page sections.
- **Links:** D-3.13-3; `src/app/(site)/arhiva/[slug]/page.tsx` §SECTIONS; `src/components/archive/SeasonStory.tsx` (comment only).

### D-3.13-2 · 2026-08-06 · The `SECTIONS` doc comment was false and is corrected, along with the staff section's inline comment
- **Status:** Accepted
- **Context:** The doc comment above `SECTIONS` still claimed „Since 3.04b **every one of them renders on every season page** (D-3.04b-1) … so the jump rail is the same five links everywhere". **3.06a reversed D-3.04b-1** — sections self-omit, and the `order` filter 100 lines below says so correctly. The inline comment on section 4 carried the same dead claim („The SECTION always renders (D-3.04b-1)"), directly above the `{present.staff && …}` guard that disproves it.
- **Decision:** Rewrite both to describe live behaviour: five sections in page order, each rendering only when it holds something, so the rail differs from page to page. Also record that `empty` is retained but **currently unread**, since nothing renders it while sections self-omit.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Fix only the doc comment the brief named* — rejected: the inline comment repeats the same falsehood, sits in a block already being edited, and would be the next reader's trap. *Delete the unused `empty` strings* — rejected: out of scope and the brief says leave them; a comment noting they are unread costs nothing and loses no copy.
- **Consequences:** Two comments now match the code. No rendered output changes.
- **Links:** D-3.04b-1 (reversed at 3.06a); D-3.13-1.

### D-3.13-3 · 2026-08-06 · The section `id`s stay `prikazna` and `trener` after the rename
- **Status:** Accepted
- **Context:** `SECTIONS.story.id` and `SECTIONS.staff.id` are URL fragments. They are the anchor targets of the jump rail and appear in any `#prikazna` / `#trener` link already shared or indexed.
- **Decision:** Leave both values at their original names and add a comment on each recording that the `id` is the section's original name, kept so existing links still resolve, and that it deliberately no longer matches the label.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Rename them to `pregled`/`igraci` for tidiness* — rejected: it buys nothing visible, breaks every `#prikazna` link already out in the world, and would need a redirect layer the site does not have.
- **Consequences:** The `id` and the label now disagree by design, which is exactly why the comment exists. Verified in the built HTML: the rendered `<section id>` set is byte-identical to `main` across all 96 season pages.
- **Links:** D-3.13-1; `src/components/archive/SeasonAnchorNav.tsx` (unchanged).

### D-3.13-4 · 2026-08-06 · Тренери and Раководство are ordered by a year DERIVED at build time, not by a new `person` field
- **Status:** Accepted (owner instruction to order them; the derivation is this phase's call)
- **Context:** The Играчи band has been ranked by appearances since 3.12, but the other two bands were alphabetical, so the club's most recent coach sat mid-list and the founding president of 1922 could appear above the man who ran the club until 2024. `person` has no service-years field: `playingYears` is deliberately empty for all 74 trainer- and president-primary people (D-3.02F-4), and `legendRank` covers players only.
- **Decision:** Derive the sort year at build time from data the dataset already holds — trainers from the **latest `season.trainer` naming them** (exact trimmed name, never a substring match), officials from the **latest four-digit year in their biography's opening line**. New pure helpers in `src/lib/people.ts`; nothing is stored and nothing is rendered.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Add a `tenureStart` field to `person`* — rejected: it needs a schema deploy plus a 74-record content pass days before the domain cutover, and it would immediately drift from `season.trainer`, whereas the derived key stays in step with it automatically. *Substring-match trainer names* — rejected: „Благој Истатов" is a substring of seven multi-name season strings and loose matching would credit seasons to the wrong man.
- **Consequences:** No schema change, no Sanity write, no new dependency. Verified against the live dataset: **46/46** trainers and **28/28** officials resolve to a year, so the null branch is a guard rather than a live case. Anyone whose year stops resolving falls to the end of their band alphabetically and nothing else moves. This is a **chronology, not a ranking** — no source ranks these two bands and none is invented.
- **Links:** D-3.02F-4; D-3.12-2; D-3.13-7; `src/lib/people.ts`; `src/app/(site)/legendi/page.tsx`.

### D-3.13-5 · 2026-08-06 · The trainer index is read in the SAME round trip as the roster
- **Status:** Accepted
- **Context:** The Тренери order needs `season.trainer` for all 68 seasons that carry one, alongside the existing person query. The brief allowed either one wrapped query or a second `client.fetch`.
- **Decision:** Wrap the existing query in an object literal returning `{ "people": …, "seasons": … }`, one fetch. The typing cost was a single extra `type LegendsData` — cheaper than a second fetch. Verified that the parent-scope `^._id` back-reference for the portrait still resolves inside the wrapper.
- **Alternatives considered:** *A second `client.fetch`* — rejected: two round trips that could observe different dataset states, and two failure paths to keep in step.
- **Consequences:** The failure path is unchanged — a throw still falls through to the empty notice, now zeroing both arrays, and never crashes the route or invents filler. `bioLead` selects only `bio[0].children[0].text`, one short line, rather than shipping the 126k-character biographies to order 28 people.
- **Links:** D-3.13-4; D-3.12-1.

### D-3.13-6 · 2026-08-06 · The bands are projected field-by-field at the client boundary instead of spread
- **Status:** Accepted
- **Context:** `LegendsBrowser` is a client component and `LegendBand` declares exactly five fields (`name`, `slug`, `role`, `playingYears`, `portrait`). The band build spread the whole roster row into it, so anything selected by the query crossed into the bundle. The brief requires `bioLead` and the derived `sortYear` to be stripped.
- **Decision:** Build the card by naming its five fields rather than spreading and deleting. This makes the requirement structural — a field cannot leak unless someone adds it to the list — instead of a strip step a later edit could drop.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Rest-destructure the two fields away* (`.map(({bioLead, sortYear, ...card}) => card)`) — rejected: it trips `@typescript-eslint/no-unused-vars` (this config does not set `ignoreRestSiblings`), and silencing that with a disable comment or an eslint config change is worse than naming five fields. *Leave the spread and strip only the two named fields* — rejected: same lint problem, and it keeps the leak-by-default shape.
- **Consequences:** `legendRank` and `careerStats` — which the spread has been carrying into the client bundle since 3.12 — stop crossing as well. **Nothing rendered changes**: `LegendCard` has only ever read the five declared fields, and the Играчи band's rendered order is byte-identical to `main`. Verified: `bioLead`, `sortYear` and the president bio opening return **zero hits** in `.next/static`.
- **Links:** D-3.09-1; D-3.12-2; `src/components/legends/LegendsBrowser.tsx` (unchanged).

### D-3.13-7 · 2026-08-06 · The brief's Раководство table and the brief's tie-break rule disagree; the RULE was implemented
- **Status:** Accepted — **owed to Ace for ratification (OV-32)**
- **Context:** The brief specifies „Equal years also break on `compareByName`", and separately supplies an expected 28-row table „produced by running the specified algorithm". For **Александар Оздоленовски** the two disagree. His term line reads „…во периодот 1991–1992.", so `tenureEndYear` gives **1992** — tying with four officials whose line reads „во 1992 година" (Васе Пиперавалиев, Јанко Мицев, Костадин Манолев, Тодор Депинов). By the specified rule he sorts **first** of the five („Александар" leads alphabetically) at position **#11**; the brief's table places him **last** of the five, at **#15**, which is only reachable by breaking ties on the term's **start** year — a second derivation the brief never specifies.
- **Decision:** Implement the written rule. Ship Оздоленовски at #11, report the divergence rather than reshape the code to reproduce a table.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Match the table by also parsing the start year and breaking ties on it descending* — rejected: it invents an unspecified derivation purely to reproduce one row, and the rule is what a later maintainer will read and rely on. *Match the table by hardcoding his position* — rejected outright; that is a fabricated ordering.
- **Consequences:** **27 of 28 rows match the brief's table exactly; row 11–15 differ only in where Оздоленовски sits.** All 46 Тренери rows match exactly. The substantive question is Ace's, not the code's: should an official whose term *ended* in 1992 but *began* in 1991 sort above or below one who served only during 1992? If Ace wants start-year tie-breaking, it is a small follow-up to `compareByRecency`.
- **Links:** D-3.13-4; OV-32.

### D-3.13-8 · 2026-08-06 · One Definition-of-Done grep cannot pass as written, and Task 2's explicit instruction was followed instead

- **Context:** The DoD requires `grep -rn "Тренер и статистика" src/app src/components` to return nothing. Task 2 of the same brief lists `src/components/archive/PersonChip.tsx` among the expected survivors and says **„Leave it."** `PersonChip.tsx` is under `src/components`, so both cannot hold.
- **Decision:** Follow Task 2's explicit scope instruction — the comment on that legacy, unrendered component is untouched — and report the DoD item as unmet-as-written with the reason. This is the D-3.10-9 precedent: a DoD grep whose path was written without accounting for a file the same brief protects.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Edit the `PersonChip.tsx` comment to satisfy the grep* — rejected: it contradicts a direct instruction in the same brief and edits a component outside this phase's scope. *Report the DoD item as passed* — rejected as false.
- **Consequences:** The real requirement — that neither old string render anywhere — **does pass**: both are gone from `SECTIONS`, and the three survivors are a Studio field title (`src/sanity/schemaTypes/season.ts`, outside both paths) and two code comments. `grep -rn "Приказна за сезоната" src/app src/components` returns nothing but the `SeasonStory.tsx` comment, which this phase updated to „Преглед".
- **Links:** D-3.10-9; Task 2 of `briefs/Part-3-Phase-13-Code.md`.

### D-3.13-9 · 2026-08-06 · Prettier was run pinned via `npx`, and its collateral reformat was reverted
- **Status:** Accepted
- **Context:** The DoD requires prettier applied, but prettier is **not a dependency** of this repo and no version is pinned anywhere. Run at 3.6.2, it reformatted two JSX arrow bodies in `SeasonStory.tsx` that this phase never touched — a file the brief scopes to „comments only".
- **Decision:** Run `npx --yes prettier@3.6.2` (which installs nothing into the project), format only the four changed files, then **revert** the two unrelated JSX collapses by hand so the diff carries only comment changes for that file.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Keep the reformat* — rejected: it puts unexplained formatting churn in a diff Lazar reviews by eye, and contradicts the brief's own scoping of that file. *Add prettier as a dev dependency and pin it* — rejected: the brief forbids any dependency change, and `package.json`/`package-lock.json` are required to be unchanged.
- **Consequences:** `package.json` and `package-lock.json` are byte-unchanged. `SeasonStory.tsx`'s diff is 4 lines, all comment. **The repo still has no pinned formatter**, so the next phase that runs a different prettier version will hit the same churn — worth pinning one day, out of scope here.
- **Links:** D-3.13-1; `CLAUDE.md` §Branch & PR rules (exact versions).

### D-3.13-10 · 2026-08-06 · Phase 3.13 was merged into `main` without the Vercel-preview review, at the owner's instruction
- **Status:** Accepted (owner instruction, recorded because it departs from a written rule)
- **Context:** `CLAUDE.md` §Branch & PR rules states **„Never merge your own PR. Lazar reviews the Vercel preview and merges"**, and D-0.00-6 records that the preview is this repo's **only** review gate — there is no automated PR reviewer. PR #43 was opened correctly and the executor declined to merge it, flagging three open items: the preview had not been reviewed by anyone (its URL was not yet posted), **OV-32** is an unresolved question for Ace that could change one rendered row, and **nobody had confirmed Lazar's working copy holds no unpushed commits** — the D-3.12-8 trap, which this phase could not test for because it ran from a fresh clone. The owner was shown all three and instructed the merge to proceed anyway.
- **Decision:** Squash-merge PR #43 into `main` without a preview review, matching the repo's existing squash-merge style.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Leave the PR open for Lazar* — the recommended route and the standing rule; rejected by the owner. *Wait for Vercel to post the preview, verify the five checklist items, then merge* — offered and rejected by the owner in favour of merging immediately.
- **Consequences:** **The merge triggers the production deploy, so the first live render of 3.13 is production** — the same consequence D-3.12-9 accepted. **No human has looked at this phase's rendered output**; the verification standing in its place is the local production build (271/271, `tsc`/lint clean), the rendered-HTML checks, and the byte-level diff against a `main` baseline build recorded in the completion report. **The three owed items survive the merge and are still owed** — OV-32 may still change the Раководство row 11–15 order, and the unpushed-commit question is now a post-merge check rather than a pre-merge one: if Lazar's Mac does hold unpushed commits, they must be rebased onto the merged `main` rather than force-pushed over it. This is the **second** consecutive phase to bypass the PR/preview gate (after D-3.12-9); the gate is now the exception rather than the rule, which is worth the owner's attention.
- **Links:** D-3.12-9; D-3.12-8; D-0.00-6; OV-32; PR #43; `CLAUDE.md` §Branch & PR rules.

### D-3.14-1 · 2026-08-07 · The officials band is „Претседатели", and it counts претседатели rather than членови
- **Status:** Accepted (owner instruction — Goran)
- **Context:** The third `/legendi` band was titled „Раководство" — the *body*, not the role — while every person in it is placed there by the `president` role and every card in it carries the chip „Претседател" (`ROLE_LABEL.president`). The band heading and the chips under it therefore named two different things. Its count noun compounded this: `BAND_COUNT_NOUN.president` was `["член","членови"]`, so the band read „28 членови" under a heading about leadership generally.
- **Decision:** Rename `BAND_TITLE.president` to „Претседатели" — the plural of the existing singular `ROLE_LABEL.president = "Претседател"`, so heading, chip and count all inflect one word — and change its count noun to `["претседател","претседатели"]`. Both live in `src/lib/people.ts`; `RoleBandGrid` reads both, so this is a single-source edit and no call site restates either string.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Keep „Раководство" and change only the count noun to „претседатели"* — rejected: it makes the disagreement worse, counting presidents under a heading that does not name them. *Keep the band as „Раководство" and split `president` into `president` + other official roles* — rejected: that is a schema change, out of scope, and no source distinguishes the roles today. *Rename the heading but leave „членови"* — rejected as the brief's own reason for pairing them.
- **Consequences:** `u-h2` uppercases, so the band renders **ПРЕТСЕДАТЕЛИ** above „28 претседатели". **The rename asserts a fact about the content**: „28 претседатели" claims all 28 people in that band were club presidents, where „28 членови" claimed only that they were officials. Nobody has checked that — it is filed as **OV-35** for Ace. If the band holds a vice-president or another official, the label is now wrong in a way the old one was not. Band membership and ordering are untouched: placement is still by `ROLE_PRIORITY` (D-2.05-2) and order still by `compareByRecency` (D-3.13-4).
- **Links:** D-2.05-2; D-3.13-4; OV-35; `src/lib/people.ts`; `src/components/legends/RoleBandGrid.tsx`.

- **Status:** Accepted (owner instruction — Goran). **Supersedes the `staff.heading` half of D-3.13-1.**
- **Context:** D-3.13-1 set `staff.heading` to „Играчи и тренер" one day earlier, and its stated reason was that the section genuinely opens with the coach's name before the roster — „a heading of „Играчи" alone above a card reading „Тренер: Благој Истатов" would misdescribe the block". The owner has since asked for „Статистика на играчи", which reintroduces exactly that mismatch.
- **Decision:** Change `SECTIONS.staff.heading` to „Статистика на играчи" and leave the section body untouched — the `<h3>` „Тренер: <име>" card and the „Состав и статистика" sub-heading both stay exactly where they are, per the brief's explicit out-of-scope list. The inline comment on the section records the mismatch and names this decision, so the next reader does not "fix" it.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Also move or drop the trainer card so the heading becomes true* — rejected: the brief puts the section body out of scope and states the owner was shown the mismatch and chose to leave it. *Argue D-3.13-1's reasoning back at the owner and keep „Играчи и тренер"* — the concern was raised in this record rather than as a blocker; the owner's instruction is one day fresher than the decision it overrides.
- **Consequences:** Renders **СТАТИСТИКА НА ИГРАЧИ** above a block whose first element names the coach — **a known, owner-accepted mismatch**, and the exact thing D-3.13-1 rejected. `id` stays `"trener"` and `label` stays „Играчи" (D-3.13-3), so `#trener` links still resolve and the rail is unchanged. The heading now shares no lead word with its rail label („Играчи" → „Статистика на играчи" still contains it, so no rail link misdescribes its target).
- **Links:** D-3.13-1 (superseded in part); D-3.13-3; D-3.14-3; `src/app/(site)/arhiva/[slug]/page.tsx` §SECTIONS.

### D-3.14-3 · 2026-08-07 · The season results heading is „Резултати и стрелци"
- **Status:** Accepted (owner instruction — Goran)
- **Context:** The results section renders `SeasonRecordList` with `variant="results"`, whose rows carry the season's scorers alongside the match results. The heading named only half of that.
- **Decision:** Change `SECTIONS.results.heading` to „Резултати и стрелци". `id` stays `"rezultati"` and `label` stays „Резултати".
- **Alternatives considered:** *Rename the rail `label` to match, as D-3.13-1 did for its two sections* — rejected: „Резултати" is the short rail form of „Резултати и стрелци" and still leads the heading it jumps to, so the D-3.13-1 principle (a rail link must not misdescribe its target) is satisfied without touching it; the brief also puts the labels out of scope. *Rename the `id` to match* — rejected for D-3.13-3's reason: it breaks every `#rezultati` link already shared.
- **Consequences:** Renders **РЕЗУЛТАТИ И СТРЕЛЦИ**. The `id`/`label`/`heading` triple now disagrees on all three values for this section, which is the pattern D-3.13-3 already established and commented. ⚠️ **Measured across the built HTML: of the 95 season pages that render a results section, 76 name at least one scorer and 19 do not** — `1922-26`, `1926-1930`, `1930-31` … `1948-49` (every pre-1949 season) plus `2012-13`. On those 19 the heading promises стрелци the section does not hold, because the book records only scores for those years. The heading is a single shared constant, so it cannot vary per season without a per-season conditional the brief does not scope. Filed as **OV-36** for the owner: either accept it, or make the heading conditional on the season actually carrying a scorer. Corroboration that the wording fits the source: several seasons' own intro line already reads „…, резултати и стрелци" verbatim from the book.
- **Links:** D-3.13-1; D-3.13-3; D-3.14-2; OV-36.

### D-3.14-4 · 2026-08-07 · Петар Андреев's role is corrected to `["player","trainer"]` — he was never a club president
- **Status:** Accepted (owner correction — Goran, 2026-08-07). **Applied and live.**
- **Context:** `person` document `f69a1977-1ddd-4201-8649-33187ad837c2` (slug `petar-andreev`) held `role: ["player","president"]`. The owner states he was never a club president and did serve as a trainer. This is the same document D-2.05-2 cites as the live proof of its placement rule.
- **Decision:** Set `role` to `["player","trainer"]` — remove `president`, add `trainer`, keep `player`. A **content** write against `production`, not a schema or code change; it took effect on the live site independently of this phase's PR (ISR `revalidate = 60`). Corroboration was **not** sought from `facts.md` or the book first: `person.role` is owner-curated content and the owner is the source of record for it, which is why this is filed as a correction rather than a research finding.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Add `trainer` and keep `president` too* — rejected: the owner's statement is that the president role is wrong, not incomplete, and leaving it would keep a false chip on the card. *Leave the data and special-case him in the UI* — rejected: it hides a content error in code and every other surface (Studio, exports, future queries) would still carry the wrong fact.
- **Consequences:** He **does not move bands** — placement is by `ROLE_PRIORITY` player > trainer > president (D-2.05-2) and he keeps `player`, so he stays under Играчи. Verified in the rebuilt HTML: he appears exactly once on `/legendi`, chips „Играч · Тренер", and `/legendi/petar-andreev` no longer renders „Претседател". Band sizes are unchanged (Играчи 86 · Тренери 46 · Претседатели 28 = 160); the page-wide chip totals moved by exactly one, Тренер 65 → 66 and Претседател 30 → 29. **The edit is durable** — nothing in the repo writes `person.role` (`bios.mjs` sets `bio`/`legendRank`, `fill-season-content.mjs` sets `story`/`results`, `classify.mjs` classifies photo folders), so no re-ingest can revert it. **The trainer service itself is uncorroborated** — the book records him primarily as a player from 1974 — filed as **OV-37** for Ace. ⚠️ Because he now holds `trainer`, he is a candidate for the `compareByRecency` trainer-year index (D-3.13-4); he is not placed in that band, so no ordering changed, but a future phase that ranks trainers will see him.
- **Links:** D-2.05-2; D-3.13-4; D-3.14-5; OV-37; `scripts/fix-andreev-role.mjs`.

### D-3.14-5 · 2026-08-07 · The role patch was applied through the authenticated Sanity MCP, not the committed script
- **Status:** Accepted (owner instruction in-session, after the executor put both routes to them)
- **Context:** The brief's Task 10 says to apply the correction with `node scripts/fix-andreev-role.mjs --commit`, and Task 11 says that if `SANITY_API_WRITE_TOKEN` is absent from `.env.local`, the executor must **not** fake it — ship the dry-run-verified script and report the write as owed. **The token is absent on this machine** (`.env.local` holds only the three public `NEXT_PUBLIC_SANITY_*` values, per D-3.13's machine notes). The script's dry run passed and printed the exact planned change. Separately, this session had an **authenticated Sanity MCP connection** (OAuth, as `DinovLazar`), which could perform the write without a token ever touching the repo — a route Task 11 did not anticipate but which matches its own parenthetical, „the orchestrator can then apply the one-field patch from the Cowork session instead".
- **Decision:** Put both routes to the owner rather than choose silently; on their instruction, apply the patch via the MCP. The MCP **cannot write published content directly** — it edits the draft — so the sequence was: read the published doc and its `_rev`; patch with `ifRevisionId` set to that `_rev` (optimistic lock, so a concurrent edit would have failed the write rather than clobbered it); verify the resulting draft held exactly `["player","trainer"]` with name and slug intact; publish; re-read under the `raw` perspective to confirm the published doc carries the new value **and that no draft was left behind**.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Follow Task 11 literally and report the write as owed* — the brief's letter, and the executor's recommendation; rejected by the owner in favour of applying it now. *Write a token into `.env.local` to run the script* — never on the table: the brief forbids faking it, and it would put a secret on this machine for a one-field edit. *Patch the draft and leave it unpublished* — rejected: the site reads `perspective: "published"` with no token (D-1.04-2), so an unpublished draft would change nothing visible while leaving a shadow copy that could later republish the old state.
- **Consequences:** The committed script is therefore **dry-run-verified but never run with `--commit`** — its write path is unexercised. That is acceptable because it is now a no-op: re-run today it reports „веќе исправено" and exits 0 (confirmed). **A draft briefly existed** for this document where none had before; it was consumed by the publish and the post-write `raw` read confirms none remains. The write bypassed the script's own guards, so the same guards were performed by hand through the MCP before publishing. **This is a production content write made from an executor session** — the first in this project — and it went live within ~60 s, before any PR review.
- **Links:** D-3.14-4; D-1.04-2; Task 10/11 of `briefs/Part-3-Phase-14-Code.md`.

### D-3.14-6 · 2026-08-07 · The script treats the already-corrected state as an idempotent no-op instead of failing its own guard
- **Status:** Accepted
- **Context:** The brief asks for a script that is both **idempotent** and that **asserts the current `role` contains `"president"`** before writing. Taken literally the two conflict: after one successful run the document no longer contains `president`, so a second run would fail the assertion and exit non-zero — which is not idempotence, it is a script that breaks on its second use.
- **Decision:** Order the checks so both hold. If `role` already deep-equals `["player","trainer"]`, print „веќе исправено" and exit **0** — the target state is not an error. Only otherwise is the `president` assertion applied, and a role array that is neither the known before-state nor the known after-state exits **1** and writes nothing. Two guards were added beyond the brief: an **unknown-role check** (`role` is a free string array in the schema, and reordering through the canonical order would silently drop a value outside the Studio list), and a **draft check** — reported honestly as *inconclusive* without a token, since an anonymous read cannot distinguish „no draft" from „not permitted to see one".
- **Alternatives considered:** *Fail hard on the second run, as the literal reading gives* — rejected: it turns a safe re-run into a false alarm, and the brief's own word for the script is „idempotent". *Skip the draft check entirely* — rejected: a draft would shadow the fix the moment anyone published it, so it is worth surfacing even when the answer is „unknown".
- **Consequences:** The script is safe to re-run by anyone at any time and reports one of three honest outcomes: already-correct, a planned change, or a refusal with the reason. It is dead code the moment the patch lands (D-3.14-5), kept as the record of what was changed and as the re-runnable check that it is still true.
- **Links:** D-3.14-4; D-3.14-5; `scripts/ingest/bios.mjs` (the pattern copied).

### D-3.14-7 · 2026-08-07 · The DoD grep for „Раководство" cannot return nothing, and was scoped to code
- **Status:** Accepted (records a brief-internal conflict, not a code fault). Direct precedent: D-3.13-8, D-3.10-9.
- **Context:** The Definition of Done requires `grep -rn "Раководство" src/` to return nothing. The project-state files live **under `src/`** (`src/_project-state/`), and `decisions.md` there is **append-only by CLAUDE.md** — it holds six historical uses of the old label, including D-3.13-4 which is about that band. This phase's own D-3.14-1 must also name the old label to record the rename at all. Seven completion reports under `src/_project-state/completions/` likewise record it as shipped history. So the grep cannot return nothing unless the repo's append-only rule is broken.
- **Decision:** Read the DoD's own parenthetical — „all user-facing strings and the code comments that named the band are updated" — as the real requirement, and scope the check to code: `grep -rn "Раководство" src/app src/components src/lib src/sanity` returns **nothing**. All five code occurrences the brief enumerates were changed. The two **live** state mirrors (`current-state.md`, `file-map.md`) were updated to the new label as part of the normal state sync. The **append-only** record (`decisions.md`) and the **historical** completion reports were left exactly as written.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Rewrite the state files and past reports so the literal grep passes* — rejected: it would falsify an append-only decision log and seven closed phase reports, breaking a core repo rule to satisfy a path glob. *Report the DoD item as passed as written* — rejected as false.
- **Consequences:** The DoD item is **met in substance and unmet as literally written**, recorded here rather than quietly passed. Nothing user-facing says „Раководство" any more. Anyone re-running the literal grep will get hits from the history files and should read this entry.
- **Links:** D-3.13-8; D-3.10-9; D-3.14-1; CLAUDE.md §Decisions (append-only).

### D-3.15-0 · 2026-08-08 · The brief's phase number (3.13) was already taken; this phase runs as 3.15
- **Status:** Accepted (executor call, reported to the owner before any code was written)
- **Context:** `briefs/Part-3-Phase-13-Code.md` describes itself as Part 3 · Phase 3.13 and states that `main` „carries phases up to and including 3.12". It does not: `main` also carries **3.13** („Преименување на две секции…", PR #43, merged 2026-08-06) and **3.14** („Четири исправки на етикети и содржина", PR #44, merged 2026-08-07). `D-3.13-1…10` and `D-3.14-1…8` already exist in this log, and both completion reports are filed. Taking the brief's number literally would have produced a second, colliding `D-3.13-*` block — the exact failure recorded at 3.11, where two sessions both claimed one number.
- **Decision:** Run the brief's content as **Phase 3.15**: branch `phase-3.15-about-socials-legends`, decision IDs `D-3.15-*`, report `Part-3-Phase-15-Completion.md`. The brief's slug, scope, copy and Definition of Done are followed unchanged — only the number moves.

- **Consequences:** The brief on disk and this phase's records disagree on the number; anyone reading `briefs/Part-3-Phase-13-Code.md` must know it shipped as 3.15. **Five further premises in that brief were also stale and are recorded where they bite** — Task 5e was already done (D-3.14-4), the band is „Претседатели" not „Раководство" (D-3.14-1), the staff bands are chronological not alphabetical (D-3.13-4), `legendRank` was already declared (only the deploy was outstanding), and no player is missing `playingYears` any more.

### D-3.15-1 · 2026-08-08 · The footer's copy line asks for permission; it does not claim ownership
- **Status:** Accepted (wording supplied verbatim by the owner)
- **Context:** The owner supplied one line for the footer: „Текстовите и фотографиите … не смеат да се преземаат … без претходна дозвола од авторот." A reviewer's instinct is to „tidy" this into a normal copyright notice („© сите права задржани"). That would be wrong, and wrong in a way that contradicts a page this site already publishes.
- **Decision:** Render the line byte-for-byte as supplied, in the footer's **bottom bar** beside the © and „Правни информации", and record the reasoning in `src/lib/facts.ts` and in `SiteFooter.tsx` so the next reader does not "fix" it.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Rewrite it as an ownership/copyright claim* — rejected: this archive publishes newspaper scans and material whose rights sit with third parties (`facts.md` §Photo publishing rights records that caveat explicitly), and `/pravni-informacii` §5 promises an **unconditional takedown on request**. A line claiming the site owns every photograph would have the site contradict its own legal page. *Put it in a fourth footer column* — rejected: it is a posture statement, not a navigation destination; the bottom bar is where the © and the legal link already live.
- **Consequences:** The line is a request, not an assertion of title — which is legally weaker and factually honest. It sits on its own row inside the bottom bar rather than as a third flex item, because at ~150 characters it would otherwise force the © line to wrap awkwardly at every width between 375 and 1280. Capped at the reading measure (62ch → 538px at 1408) so it does not run the full container width. **Still owed: a native-speaker read** (OV-38).
- **Links:** `/pravni-informacii` §5; `facts.md` §Photo publishing rights; `CONTENT_PERMISSION_NOTICE`; OV-38.

### D-3.15-2 · 2026-08-08 · The „фан-страница" framing of the Facebook link is retired, and both socials render on both surfaces
- **Status:** Accepted — **Supersedes D-3.12-6** (both halves)
- **Context:** D-3.12-6 labelled the Facebook link „Фејсбук страница на навивачите" and scoped it to the footer, reasoning that this archive does not run the page and that a bare „Фејсбук" would falsely claim it. **The „За нас" copy the owner supplied for this same phase states the opposite outright:** Аце Стојанов is the author and editor of this site, of the FB profile *FK Belasica 1956-2006*, and of the book. The Instagram handle `belasica1956.2006` is the same identity.
- **Decision:** Retire the fan-page label. Render both profiles as icon links, under „Следете нѐ", on **both** surfaces — the footer and `/kontakt` — from one shared `SocialLinks` component reading one set of `facts.md` constants.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Keep the „навивачите" label* — rejected: the site would call the profile somebody else's fan page on one page and name its author on another. *Keep the footer-only scope of D-3.12-6* — rejected: it was scoped that way because only one unverified link existed; with both profiles VERIFIED and attributed, leaving `/kontakt` showing a „no profiles" chip while the footer of the same page linked them was the incoherence PL-15 had been flagging since 2.07. *Duplicate the markup on each page* — rejected: two copies of the hrefs and accessible names drift the first time one changes.
- **Consequences:** PL-15 closes on both surfaces at once. The site now carries exactly two external links. The old `FACEBOOK_FAN_PAGE` / `FACEBOOK_FAN_PAGE_LABEL` constants are gone, replaced by `FACEBOOK_URL`, `INSTAGRAM_URL` and `SOCIAL_LINKS`.
- **Links:** D-3.12-6 (superseded); D-2.07-4; PL-15; `src/components/SocialLinks.tsx`; `src/lib/facts.ts`.

### D-3.15-3 · 2026-08-08 · The Instagram URL's tracking query string is stripped
- **Status:** Accepted
- **Context:** The address the owner supplied was `https://www.instagram.com/belasica1956.2006?utm_source=qr&igsh=N2MxeWw4OHRocGxh` — the form Instagram generates when a profile is reached by scanning its QR code.
- **Decision:** Store and render the bare `https://www.instagram.com/belasica1956.2006`.
- **Alternatives considered:** *Keep the URL exactly as supplied* — rejected: `utm_source=qr` and `igsh=…` are a scan-attribution payload, not part of the address. Shipping them would tell Instagram that every visitor from this site arrived by scanning a QR code, which is false, and would put an opaque session-ish token in a public repo for no gain.
- **Consequences:** The bare URL resolves to the same profile (verified: the built HTML carries no `?` in either social href). If the owner ever wants the archive's referrals attributed, that is a deliberate `utm_source=belasicahistory` decision, not this leftover.
- **Links:** D-3.15-2; `INSTAGRAM_URL`.

### D-3.15-4 · 2026-08-08 · `legendAppearances` is a string, not a number
- **Status:** Accepted (specified by the brief; recorded because it will look like a modelling mistake later)
- **Context:** The new field holds the appearance count printed beside a legend's rank. The obvious type is `number`. The book does not cooperate: **nine of the eighty ranked players are printed with a range** („120–135", „100–103") rather than a figure — which is the same fact that made 3.12 rank on `legendRank` instead of on `careerStats.appearances` (D-3.12-2).
- **Decision:** Declare `person.legendAppearances` as a `string`, storing the book's printed value verbatim with an en dash for ranges.
- **Alternatives considered:** *A `number` field* — rejected: it cannot hold „120–135" at all, so those nine would be blank or silently rounded to one end of their span, inventing a statistic. *Two numbers (`appearancesMin`/`appearancesMax`)* — rejected: it models a precision the book does not have, needs display logic to collapse back to „120–135", and makes the common single-value case awkward. *Reuse `careerStats.appearances`* — rejected: different provenance and a different job (D-2.01-3); see D-3.15-12.
- **Consequences:** The field cannot be sorted or summed numerically — correct, because it is a transcription, not a statistic. Sorting stays on `legendRank`, which is what the book's own ordering is. A future editor typing „555 натпревари" into the field would render that string literally; the Studio description gives the expected format.
- **Links:** D-3.12-2; D-2.01-3; D-3.15-12; `src/sanity/schemaTypes/person.ts`.

### D-3.15-5 · 2026-08-08 · A ranked player with no printed count renders rank + name and nothing else
- **Status:** Accepted
- **Context:** Only **32 of the 80** ranked players have a count to show (see D-3.15-12). The other 48 have a rank and no figure. Something has to render for them.
- **Decision:** Show the rank and the name, and omit the count entirely — no chip, no `0`, no dash, no „—". The count element is not emitted at all unless both `legendRank` and `legendAppearances` are present.
- **Alternatives considered:** *A `[PLACEHOLDER: настапи]` chip* — rejected: 48 chips on one page is not a signal, it is noise, and the register exists for facts the site is waiting on, not for a field the source may never fill. *A dash or `0`* — rejected: `0` is a false statistic and a dash reads as „zero appearances" to a screen reader. *Hide the rank too when the count is missing* — rejected: the rank is the band's ordering key and is true independently of the count.
- **Consequences:** The Играчи band reads „1. … 555", „2. … 383", then „3. Ристо Панов" with no number, and counts resume at 51. That gap is visible and is the point — it shows exactly where the archive's transcription stops rather than papering over it. **Raised as OV-39.**
- **Links:** D-2.02-3; D-2.08-2; D-3.15-12; OV-39; `src/components/legends/LegendCard.tsx`.
